005 ATGLEN DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.
Governor

050 CALN 2 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY

COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

165 E COVENTRY 2 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

207 EASTTOWN 5 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

244 E GOSHEN 2 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

261 W GOSHEN N-3 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

300 HONEY BROOK TOWNSHIP 1 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

335 LONDONDERRY DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

375 E NANTMEAL DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

415 OXFORD E DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

456 PENNSBURY S-1 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

535 SADSBURY N DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

575 SPRING CITY 4 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

616 TREDYFFRIN M-3 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.
Governor
Vote for One
Josh Shapiro
Montgomery County

653 UWCHLAN 1 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.
Governor

683 E VINCENT 4 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

730 WESTTOWN 3 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

760 W WHITELAND 2 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.
Governor

790 WILLISTOWN N-5 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.

846 WEST CHESTER 5 DEM
OFFICIAL CHESTER COUNTY
GENERAL PRIMARY ELECTION DEMOCRATIC BALLOT
COUNTY OF CHESTER, COMMONWEALTH OF PENNSYLVANIA
May 19, 2026
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. TO VOTE YOU MUST COMPLETELY DARKEN THE OVAL ( ) TO THE LEFT OF YOUR CHOICE. An oval ( ) darkened to the left of the name
of any candidate indicates a vote for that candidate.
2. To cast a write-in vote for a person whose name is not on the ballot, you must darken the oval ( ) to the left of the line provided, and write or print
the name in the blank space provided for that purpose.
3. Use only black or blue ink pen.
4. If you make a mistake DO NOT ERASE. Ask for a new ballot.
5. WARNING:If you receive an absentee or mail-in ballot and return your voted ballot by the deadline, you may not vote at your polling place on
Election Day. If you are unable to return your voted absentee or mail-in ballot by the deadline, you may only vote a provisional ballot at your polling
place on Election Day, unless you surrender your absentee or mail-in ballot and declaration envelope to the Judge of Elections to be voided to vote
by regular ballot.
Governor
Vote for One
Josh Shapiro
